2. 本规则的背景和目的
2. Background and Purpose of the Rule
国土安全部旨在通过将所有福利申请从使用纸质表格转变为使用电子申报方式,以实现美国公民及移民服务局运营的现代化。美国公民及移民服务局目前鼓励人们在线申报,但并未强制要求。对于以纸质表格形式收到的申请,该局在收到后会将纸质福利申请数字化。此流程存在重复且成本高昂的问题,因为它既需要人工为纸质表格办理收件手续,又需要扫描并将信息录入数字系统。国土安全部将“电子申报”定义为:以美国公民及移民服务局提供并批准的任何方式,以电子方式提交福利申请、佐证证据、文件、通知和通信,包括通过在线填写表格、通过网络门户、电子接口完成填写,或通过已批准的在线账户上传已填写完成的表格的PDF文件。
DHS seeks to modernize USCIS operations by shifting from using paper forms to using e-filing for all benefit requests. USCIS currently encourages people to file online but does not require them to do so. For submissions received on paper forms, the agency digitizes the paper benefit requests upon receiving them. This process is duplicative and costly as it requires manual receipting paper forms, as well as scanning and entering information into the digital systems. DHS defines “e-file” as submission of a benefit request, supporting evidence, documents, notices, and communication electronically in any manner made available and approved by USCIS, including by completing the form online, in a web portal, electronic interface, or by uploading a PDF of the completed form through an approved online account.
为减少因使用纸质表格而产生的低效现象,并最终实现向完全电子化环境的过渡,美国公民及移民服务局希望制定相关举措,使其能够摆脱既允许某一表格在线提交、又要求相关或附属表格以纸质方式提交的做法。强制实行电子申报将使美国公民及移民服务局能够取消依赖纸质的系统,实现流程集中化,并针对所有福利申请扩大自动化功能。这些效益将通过消除低效流程来降低人力成本并缩短裁决时间。数字化解决方案还将在整个机构范围内增强安全性、裁决完整性和申请人服务水平。此外,为与遵守表格说明中关于签名和所需数据要素的要求保持一致,国土安全部正在对8 CFR 103.2(a)(1)(i)进行程序性和技术性修订,以更通俗的语言重申,申请必须按照表格说明的要求完成。美国公民及移民服务局确保表格说明与法定和法规标准保持一致。以此方式厘清法规文本不会改变表格说明的效力,也不会导致被驳回的申请数量增加或减少。
To reduce the inefficiencies arising from using paper forms and ultimately transition to a fully electronic environment, USCIS wants to develop initiatives allowing it to move away from systems that permit online submission of one form while requiring related or ancillary forms to be filed on paper. Mandating e-filing would allow USCIS to eliminate paper dependent systems, centralize processes, and expand automated functions for all benefit requests. These benefits would reduce labor costs and adjudication times by eliminating inefficient processes. Digital solutions would also enhance security, adjudicative integrity, and applicant services on an agency-wide basis. Moreover, consistent with the requirements to follow form instructions regarding signatures and required data elements, DHS is making procedural and technical amendments to 8 CFR 103.2(a)(1)(i) by restating in plainer language that requests must be completed as required by form instructions. USCIS ensures that form instructions are consistent with statutory and regulatory criteria. Clarifying the regulatory text in this manner will not change the effect of form instructions or result in fewer or more requests being rejected.
基于上述原因,向完全电子化环境的过渡将提升美国公民及移民服务局的使命履行、服务水平和管理职责。
For these reasons, moving to a fully electronic environment would enhance the mission, service, and stewardship of USCIS.
本规则的成本和收益将聚焦于使用将被纳入强制电子申报范围的美国公民及移民服务局表格申请福利的申请人,以及美国公民及移民服务局所承担的成本和收益。国土安全部依据2016财年至2025财年期间使用各类美国公民及移民服务局表格提出福利申请的申请人数量,对受影响申请人的数量进行估算。[144]
The costs and benefits of this rule would focus on requestors applying for benefits using the USCIS forms that would be subject to mandatory e-filing as well as costs and benefits to USCIS. DHS bases the estimation of the number of affected requestors on the number of requestors who filed for benefit requests from FY 2016 to FY 2025 using various USCIS forms.[144]
3. 基线和申请群体
3. Baseline and Population
本规则将影响使用可供电子申请的USCIS表格申请移民福利的申请人。受本规则影响的估计人数是基于2016财年至2025财年期间为申请移民福利而提交的表格总数得出的。在评估本规则的影响时,美国国土安全部(DHS)采用“不采取行动”基线,该基线反映了现行法规和申请做法下的现状,即申请人和申请人可根据表格资格和申请人偏好选择以电子方式或纸质方式提交福利申请。若无本规则,DHS将继续接受并处理纸质申请,且在可电子申请的情况下,电子申请仍为自愿选择。DHS将本规则的影响估计为本规则实施后的结果与“不采取行动”基线之间的差异,该基线反映了在没有本规则的情况下将发生的情况。此项比较显示了本规则如何改变成本、收益和转移支付。在基线情形下,部分申请人自愿以电子方式提交申请,而其他申请人则以纸质方式提交;在本规则下,USCIS可能要求申请人以电子方式提交福利申请,同时为申请人提供申请豁免电子申请要求的机会。本分析衡量的是增量影响,而非无论是否采取监管行动都会发生的变化。
This rule would affect requestors applying for immigration benefits using the USCIS forms available for e-filing. The estimated population affected by the rule is based on the total number of forms filed for immigration benefits in FY 2016 to FY 2025. In estimating the impacts of this rule, DHS uses a no-action baseline that reflects the current state of the world under existing regulations and filing practices, in which applicants and petitioners may submit benefit requests either electronically or on paper, depending on form eligibility and filer preference. Absent this rule, DHS would continue to accept and process paper filings, and e-filing would remain voluntary where available. DHS estimates the impacts of the rule as the differences between outcomes under the rule and a no-action baseline, which reflects what would occur in the absence of the rule. This comparison shows how the rule changes costs, benefits, and transfers. Under the baseline, some requestors voluntarily submit electronically while others file on paper; under this rule, USCIS may require requestors to e-file a benefit request, while providing requestors an opportunity to seek a waiver of the e-filing requirement. The analysis measures the incremental effects rather than changes that would occur regardless of regulatory action.
与“不采取行动”基线保持一致,本分析使用历史受理数据来反映现行法规下观察到的申请行为。由于电子申请目前为自愿性质,过去按申请渠道划分的受理量为在无监管变化情况下申请人选择提供了最佳证据。表11汇总了过去10个财政年度(2016财年至2025财年)USCIS所有申请受理量,按纸质和电子提交方式分类,并作为估算基线申请模式和识别受电子申请强制要求新影响人群的依据。
Consistent with the no-action baseline, this analysis uses historical receipt data to reflect observed filing behavior under current regulations. Because e-filing is currently voluntary, past receipt volumes by filing channel provide the best evidence of filer choices absent regulatory change. Table 11 summarizes all USCIS application receipt volumes for the past 10 fiscal years (FY 2016-FY 2025), separated into paper and electronic submissions, and serves as the basis for estimating baseline filing patterns and identifying the population newly affected by the e-filing mandate.
表11显示,在2016财年至2025财年期间,申请方式明显从纸质申请转向电子申请。纸质申请仍是主要渠道,但其占受理量的比例已从2016财年的91%下降至2025财年的57%,而同期电子申请比例则从9%上升至43%。USCIS年度受理量从2016财年的约900万件上升至2024财年的峰值1580万件,其中2020财年因疫情影响出现下降。 (印刷页第51956页)在2022财年至2023财年之间出现明显跃升,这主要是由I-134A表、I-589表、I-765表和I-821表受理量激增所致。[145]PDFi的使用仅在2024财年和2025财年出现,且始终远低于总受理量的1%。这些趋势表明,在“不采取行动”基线下,电子申请的采用率正在上升,但绝大多数申请人仍使用纸质申请方式。强制要求电子申请将主要影响这一持续使用纸质申请的人群。
Table 11 shows a clear shift from paper filing to e-filing over the period FY 2016-FY 2025. Paper filings remain the dominant channel but have declined from 91 percent of receipts in FY 2016 to 57 percent in FY 2025, while e-filing rose from 9 percent to 43 percent over the same period. USCIS annual receipts rose from approximately 9 million in FY 2016 to a peak of 15.8 million in FY 2024, with a dip in FY 2020 consistent with pandemic effects. A pronounced ( printed page 51956) jump between FY 2022 and FY 2023 mostly driven by spikes in receipts for Forms I-134A, I-589, I-765, and I-821.[145] Use of PDFi emerges only in FY 2024 and FY 2025 and remains well under 1 percent of total receipts. These trends indicate that, under the no action baseline, e-filing adoption is increasing but a substantial majority of requestors still use paper. A mandate would primarily affect this persistent paper-filing population.
DHS使用表11中2016财年至2025财年这10年期间的历史数据得出的估计值来估算受影响人群的基线。在表12中,DHS估算了每种福利申请在此10年期间受理总量的历史平均值,并将其用作预计年度受理量。我们对在本分析期间内已可通过引导式在线申请或PDFi方式进行电子申请的所有福利申请均采用此方法。DHS采用10年历史平均值,原因是最近三年的申请量因特定的、非经常性的政策变化而出现显著的异常激增。若主要依赖这些近期年份的数据,将高估未来对移民福利的需求。采用10年期数据可平滑这些由政策驱动的波动,从而为受影响人群提供更稳定、更具代表性的估计。
DHS uses estimates derived from the historical data in Table 11 for the 10-year period from FY 2016 through FY 2025 to estimate the baseline affected population. In Table 12, DHS estimates the historical 10-year average of total receipts for each benefit request and uses it as the projected annual receipts. We do this for all benefit requests currently available for e-filing, either through guided online filing or PDFi, during this analysis period. DHS uses a 10-year historical average because the most recent three years contain significant, atypical spikes in filing volumes driven by specific, non-recurring policy changes. Relying mostly on those recent years would overestimate future demand for immigration benefits. Using a 10-year period smooths these policy-driven fluctuations and provides a more stable and representative estimate of the affected population.
一旦强制电子申请生效,申请移民福利的申请人将在被要求以电子方式而非纸质方式提交申请的范围内受到影响。为估算需要从纸质申请转为电子申请的申请人数量,DHS将最近一个完整财政年度(2025财年)的纸质申请比例用作“不采取行动”基线。表12显示,本规则估计受影响人群每年约为600万名申请人。为估算本规则的经济影响,DHS对自2027财年起10年期间内受影响人群进行了预测。
Once mandatory e-filing takes effect, requestors filing immigration benefit requests will be affected to the extent they are required to submit their requests electronically rather than by submitting papers. To estimate the number of requestors required to switch from paper to e-filing, DHS uses as the no-action baseline the most recent complete fiscal year (FY 2025) percentage of paper filings. Table 12 shows that the estimated affected population of the rule is approximately 6 million petitioners annually. To estimate the economic impacts of this rule, DHS projects the affected population over the 10-year period beginning in FY 2027.
此外,每年约有120万份与福利申请相关的受理案件尚不能通过电子方式提交。这些福利申请只有在美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)使其可以电子方式提交,并且在该电子提交方式已可使用至少180天之后,才会受到本规则所确立的电子提交要求以及相关豁免和费用规定的约束。截至本次规则制定时,USCIS尚未公布将电子提交能力扩展至这些剩余福利申请的实施时间表。与这些福利申请相关的任何影响,包括潜在的成本节约、额外成本和转移支付,均未计入本分析中所列的量化收益和成本。
Additionally, there are approximately 1.2 million annual receipts associated with benefit requests that are not yet available for e-filing. These benefit requests would become subject to the e-filing requirement, and the associated waiver and fee provisions established by this rule, only after USCIS makes them available for electronic submission and they have been available for at least 180 days. As of this rulemaking, USCIS has not made public an implementation schedule to extend e-filing capability to these remaining benefit requests. Any associated impacts, including potential costs savings, additional costs, and transfers, on these benefit requests are not included in the quantified benefits and costs presented in this analysis.
本规则规定了申请豁免电子提交要求的程序。该豁免程序确保美国国土安全部(DHS)遵守《21世纪综合数字体验法案》(21st Century IDEA Act,公法第115-336号,第(4)(e)条,2018年12月20日)的规定,该法要求每个行政机关维持一种可通过当面、纸质或其他方式完成数字服务的无障碍途径,使无法使用数字服务的个人不会因此被剥夺或阻碍获取这些数字服务的机会。DHS估计,在预计受强制电子提交要求影响的年度申请人总数中,将有4%的申请人使用新的USCIS表格I-936申请豁免。
This rule provides a process to request a waiver of the e-filing requirement. This waiver process ensures DHS complies with the 21st Century IDEA Act, Public Law 115-336, sec. (4)(e) (Dec. 20, 2018), which requires each executive agency to maintain an accessible method of completing digital services through in-person, paper-based, or other means, such that individuals without the ability to use digital services are not deprived of or impeded in access to those digital services. DHS estimates that, among the total projected annual number of requestors who would be affected by the mandatory e-filing requirements, 4 percent of the requestors would apply for a waiver using new USCIS Form I-936.
2025年10月,USCIS开始实施强制性在线缴费,申请人须通过自动清算所(ACH)或信用卡支付申请费。截至2025年12月,约96%的申请人遵守了该要求,截至2026年2月,该比例约为99%。[146](第51957页印刷版)尽管电子支付和电子提交在技术要求上有所不同,但这两项政策都涉及从传统合规机制向电子处理系统的过渡。因此,DHS将强制在线缴费初期观察到的不合规率(4%)作为估算可能寻求强制电子提交豁免的申请人比例的合理且保守的替代指标。DHS采用96%的合规率而非99%,作为一种保守估计。这意味着更高的豁免申请数量,可能高估实际提交的豁免申请数量,从而避免高估向电子提交过渡所带来的收益。表12反映了豁免人群的百分比,受影响人群的估算使用了96%的合规率。DHS估计,目前可通过电子方式提交的表格中,每年预计申请豁免的人数为248,905人。[147]为保持分析的一致性,DHS假设,除可能寻求豁免的人群外,受影响人群均可以访问互联网并能够以电子方式提交表格。
In October 2025, USCIS implemented mandatory online payment for filing fees through either an Automated Clearing House (ACH) or credit card payment, approximately 96 percent of filers complied with the requirements by December 2025 and approximately 99 percent by February 2026.[146] While ( printed page 51957) electronic payment and e-filing impose different technical requirements, both policies involve transitioning from legacy compliance mechanisms to electronic processing system. Therefore, DHS uses observed initial non-compliance rate (4 percent) from mandatory online payment as a reasonable and conservative proxy for estimating the percentage of requestors who may seek a waiver from mandatory e-filing. DHS uses the 96 percent compliance rate, rather than 99 percent, as a conservative estimate. This implies a higher waiver filing, which is likely to overstate the number of waivers filed and avoids overstating the benefits of the transition to e-filing. Table 12 reflects the percentage of the waiver population, and the estimated affected population is calculated using 96 percent compliance rate. DHS estimates that the annual projected population of requestors who would apply for a waiver would be 248,905 for the forms currently available electronically.[147] For consistency in the analysis, DHS assumes that, excluding the population that may seek a waiver, the affected population has internet access and is able to e-file forms.
(第51958页印刷版)
( printed page 51958)
4. 经济影响
4. Economic Impact
a. 可量化的影响
a. Quantifiable Impact
成本节约
Cost Savings
与不采取行动的基准情形相比,本规则将为申请人带来成本节约,具体体现在无需提交纸质福利申请、无需因申请存在缺陷或信息缺失而重新提交被退回的纸质表格,以及申请人因此节省的时间成本。本规则还将带来成本节约,因为申请人无需再通过邮政快递方式将包含纸质福利申请表格的邮包寄送至USCIS进行处理。DHS估计,每位申请人平均可因不再向USCIS提交纸质邮包而节省11.95美元的邮资成本。[148] DHS将该平均每份邮包的邮寄成本应用于表12所列的年度受影响人群5,973,727份申请,估算得出每年因不再邮寄含纸质福利申请的邮包而节省的成本约为7100万美元。
Compared to the no-action baseline, this rule will result in cost savings to requestors from not filing paper-based benefit requests, not having to re-submit paper forms rejected due to filing deficiencies or missing information, and time burden cost savings to requestors. This rule will result in cost savings from not sending a package containing paper-based benefit request forms via mail couriers to USCIS for processing. DHS estimates that each requestor would save an average of $11.95 in postage costs from not submitting the paper-based package to USCIS.[148] DHS applies the averagemailing cost per package to the annual affected population of 5,973,727 filings (Table 12) to estimate approximately $71 million in annual cost savings from no longer mailing packages containing paper-based benefit requests.
本规则还将因无需重新提交最终因申请缺陷而被拒绝的纸质表格而节省成本,此类缺陷包括(印刷页51959)提交费用金额不正确、缺少签字、使用过期的申请表格,或提交的福利申请未经签署或缺少必要的数据要素。美国国土安全部(DHS)估计,在当前的申请制度下,平均每年有1,005,297份纸质福利申请因此被拒绝。[149] 纸质福利申请随后需要在纠正申请缺陷后重新提交。强制通过PDFi进行电子申请并不会消除重新申请的必要性,因为美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)在受理阶段仍会审查PDFi提交材料,如果不符合规定的受理标准,仍可能予以拒收。因此,目前因申请缺陷而被拒绝的1,005,297份纸质福利申请并非全部都能带来成本节省。美国国土安全部估计,在强制电子申请制度下,这些被拒绝的申请中有996,523份[150]将不再需要重新提交,因为使用引导式在线申请界面的申请人能够在提交前立即纠正申请缺陷,而无需等待拒收通知后再重新提交纸质福利申请。美国国土安全部通过将996,523份避免的重新申请数量乘以每份邮寄的平均费用(11.95美元),估算出因避免重新提交因申请缺陷或信息缺失而被拒绝的纸质表格所带来的成本节省,约为1200万美元。
This rule will also result in cost savings from not having to re-file paper-based forms that are ultimately rejected due to filing deficiencies, such as ( printed page 51959) submitting an incorrect fee amount, lack of signature, use of outdated benefit request forms, or submission of benefit requests not executed or missing required data elements. DHS estimates that in the current filing system, on average, 1,005,297 paper-based benefit requests are rejected annually.[149] Paper-based benefit requests would then need to be re-filed after correcting the filing deficiencies. Mandating e-filing via PDFi would not eliminate the need to refile because USCIS still reviews PDFi submissions at intake and may reject them if they do not meet defined acceptance criteria. Therefore, not all the 1,005,297 paper-based benefit requests currently rejected for filing deficiencies would result in cost savings. DHS estimates that, under mandatory e-filing, 996,523 of these rejected requests [150] would no longer require refiling because filers using the guided online filing interface would be able to correct filing deficiencies immediately before submission, rather than waiting for a rejection notice and refiling their paper-based benefit requests. DHS estimates cost savings from avoided re-filings of paper forms rejected due to filing deficiencies or missing information by multiplying 996,523 avoided re-filings by the averagemailing cost per package ($11.95), resulting in approximately $12 million in savings.
总体而言,美国国土安全部估计,与纸质申请相比,电子申请减少了申请人完成并提交表格所需的平均时间。这些估计考虑了引导式电子申请可实现的时间节省,以及其他效率提升因素,例如免除打印、装订、包装和邮寄等活动,并提供电子验证核查和自动数据录入等电子功能。
Overall, DHS estimates that e-filing reduces the average time a requestor requires to complete and submit a form compared to paper-based filing. These estimates consider the time savings that guided e-filing can achieve plus other efficiencies such as eliminating printing, assembling, packaging, and mailing activities as well as providing electronic features such as validation checks and automated data entry.
引导式工作流程电子申请与PDFi申请的每份回复估计时间负担有所不同。例如,引导式工作流程电子申请具有提示功能,可使信息收集过程更加流畅,并能更快地引导申请人跳过无需回答的信息收集部分,而PDFi申请则要求使用与纸质申请相同的PDF版本表格,该表格必须填写完毕并作为文件上传提交。这种缺乏引导式工作流程的情况限制了PDFi申请人实现与引导式工作流程申请人相同潜在节省的能力。
Estimated time burden per response for guided workflow e-filing and for PDFi filing differ. For example, guided workflow e-filing has prompts that result in a more fluid flow through the information collection process and may direct a respondent more quickly through areas of an information collection where questions do not require a response, whereas the PDFi filing requires the PDF version of the same form used for paper filing, which must be completed and submitted as a document upload. This lack of guided workflow limits the ability for PDFi filers to realize the same potential savings as a guided workflow filer.
表13列出了按申请渠道划分的估计时间负担差异。美国国土安全部将每次申请的估计时间负担节省视为申请人机会成本的减少。总时间负担成本节省的计算方法为:每次申请节省的时间乘以从纸质转为电子申请的申请数量,再乘以报酬率。
Table 13 presents estimated time burden difference by filing channel. DHS considers estimated time burden savings per filing as a reduction in opportunity cost to requestors. The total time burden cost savings are calculated as the product of the time saved per filing, the number of filings shifting from paper to electronic, and compensation rate.

为估算额外时间负担节省的机会成本,美国国土安全部假设有四类不同的申请人,按移民福利申请的提交人区分:个人、人力资源专员、内部律师和外聘律师。大多数表格由个人提交,但基于就业的移民申请,例如I-129表、I-907表、I-140表和I-485J表,则由雇主(如人力资源专员)提交。美国国土安全部假设,随附提交G-28表(《律师或经认可代表出庭通知书》)的申请是由内部律师或外聘律师准备的,而未随附提交G-28表的申请则是由个人或人力资源专员提交的。表14列出了不同类型申请人的每小时总报酬,该数据基于其每小时平均工资率及适用的福利与工资乘数计算得出。
To estimate the opportunity cost of additional time burden savings, DHS assumes four distinct types of requestors, differentiated by who files the immigration benefits: individual, HR specialist, in-house attorney, and outsourced attorney. Most forms are filed by individuals, but employment-based immigration requests—such as Forms I-129, I-907, I-140, and I-485J—are filed by employers such as HR specialists. DHS assumes that requests submitted with a Form G-28, Notice of Entry of Appearance as Attorney or Accredited Representative, are prepared by an in-house attorney, or outside attorney, and that requests submitted without a Form G-28 are submitted by individuals or HR specialists. 表15列出了从纸质申请转为电子申请的受益申请人因减少时间负担而产生的预计年度成本节约。由于时间价值因申请人类型而异,美国国土安全部(DHS)根据受益申请书是由个人/雇主的人力资源专员直接提交,还是由代表申请人行事的内部律师或外聘律师提交,采用不同的薪酬费率来估算此类节约。通过将相应的每小时薪酬费率应用于以电子方式而非纸质方式准备和提交表格所需工时的减少量,DHS估计,在全部受影响群体中,因减少时间负担而产生的年度总成本节约约为4.5亿美元。

Because the value of time differs by requestor type, DHS estimates these savings using different compensation rates depending on whether the benefit request is filed directly by an individual/HR specialist for employer or by an in-house attorney or outsourced attorney acting on the requestor's behalf. By applying the appropriate hourly compensation rates to the reduction in hours required to prepare and submit forms electronically rather than on paper, DHS estimates total annual cost savings of approximately $450 million from reduced time burdens across all estimated affected population.

成本
Costs
本规则允许申请人因不当困难而申请豁免电子申请要求。DHS未对可使申请人有资格获得豁免的因素作出明确界定,但此类因素通常包括地理位置、社会经济状况以及公共技术资源的可获得性。申请人将使用新创建的I-936表格提交豁免申请。除需证明电子申请会造成不当困难外,寻求豁免的申请人还将承担与填写和提交I-936表格相关的额外时间和成本。
This rule allows requestors to request a waiver of the e-filing requirement because of undue hardship. DHS is not defining the factors that would qualify a requestor for a waiver, but such factors would generally include geographical location, socioeconomic conditions, and the availability of public technology resources. Requestors will submit the request for a waiver using the newly created Form I-936. In addition to demonstrating that e-filing would cause undue hardship, requestors seeking a waiver would also incur the additional time and cost associated with completing and submitting Form I-936.
DHS将强制在线支付要求所观察到的4%豁免率作为估算可能符合强制电子申请豁免资格的申请人比例的合理且保守的替代指标。将此比率应用于受影响的申请群体,DHS估计,每年将有248,905名申请人针对目前可通过电子方式提交的表格申请豁免,相关内容已在第V.B.3节“基线与群体”中讨论。在本规则中,DHS为提交I-936表格申请电子申请豁免的申请人设定了25美元的申请费。收取该费用是为了弥补部分受理和审查豁免申请的成本,此部分内容将在前言第V.B.4.c节“联邦政府的成本、成本节约及收益”中单独讨论。DHS估计,受影响群体中约有4%将提交豁免申请。[151] 在这些申请人中,DHS估计有6%将使用I-912表格“申请费豁免请求”申请费用豁免,因此无需缴纳该25美元费用。[152] 因此,DHS估计94%的豁免申请人将缴纳该费用,产生约600万美元的年度费用收入。[153] 该金额代表寻求电子申请豁免的申请人所承担的年度成本。
DHS uses the observed waiver rate of 4 percent from the mandatory online payment requirement as a reasonable and conservative proxy for estimating the share of requestors who may qualify for an exemption from mandatory e-filing. Applying this rate to the affected filing population, DHS estimates that 248,905 requestors per year would apply for a waiver for the forms currently available electronically, as discussed in Section V.B.3. Baseline and Population. In this rule, DHS is establishing a $25 filing fee for requestors submitting the Form I-936 to request a waiver of the e-filing requirement. The fee is collected to recover some of the costs of intake and review of the waiver request, which will be discussed separately in section V.B.4.c. of the preamble, Costs, Cost Savings, and Benefits to the Federal Government. DHS estimates that approximately 4 percent of the affected population will submit a waiver request.[151] Among those requestors, DHS estimates that 6 percent will request a fee waiver using Form I-912, Request for Fee Waiver, and therefore will not pay the $25 fee.[152] As a result, DHS estimates that 94 percent of waiver filers will remit the fee, producing approximately $6 million in annual fee payments.[153] This amount represents an annual cost to the requestors seeking a waiver of the e-filing requirement.
寻求豁免的申请人所承担的成本包括填写I-936表格所需时间负担的机会成本,以及邮寄已完成表格的邮资成本。根据表14中个人每小时47.36美元的薪酬费率,以及填写I-936表格所需的预计30分钟(0.5小时)时间负担,DHS估计机会成本约为每年600万美元。DHS进一步估计,邮寄豁免申请的邮资成本约为每年300万美元。[154] 因此,豁免申请人所承担的年度总成本约为1500万美元。
The cost to requestors seeking a waiver consists of the opportunity cost of the time burden required to complete Form I-936 and the postage cost to mail the completed form. Using the hourly compensation rate for individuals of $47.36 from Table 14 and estimated time burden of 30 minutes (0.5 hours) to complete Form I-936, DHS estimates an opportunity cost of approximately $6 million per year. DHS further estimates postage costs of about $3 million per year for mailing the waiver requests.[154] In total, the annual cost to waiver requestors is therefore approximately $15 million.
DHS还量化了在没有本规则的情况下仍倾向于继续以纸质方式提交申请的申请人所承担的成本。在纸质申请和电子申请两种方式均可选择、且电子申请人可获得50美元折扣的情况下,一些申请人仍然选择以纸质方式提交申请,从而放弃了这50美元的折扣。对于在提交申请时已知晓并能够使用这两种方式的申请人而言,这种行为表明,他们对熟悉的纸质流程及相关非货币利益所赋予的价值至少为每次申请50美元。DHS认识到,并非所有申请人在提交申请时都清楚地获得这一选择或能够切实使用这一选择,因此该金额可能无法完全反映其成本。此外,DHS无法可靠地估算哪些申请人实际上获得了电子申请选项及折扣信息,因此无法就受影响人群对此项成本进行汇总。根据本规则,倾向于纸质申请且无法证明符合电子申请要求豁免资格的申请人,将不再拥有以纸质方式提交福利申请的选择,而必须以电子方式提交申请,从而导致上述损失。因此,对于在纸质申请与电子申请之间拥有实质性选择权的申请人而言,DHS将50美元视为强制电子申请所产生的每次申请成本的保守估计值。
DHS also quantifies a cost to requestors who would have preferred to continue filing by paper in the absence of this rule. When both paper and e-filing options were available and electronic filers received a $50 discount, some requestors nonetheless chose to file by paper, thereby forgoing the $50 discount. For requestors who were aware of and could access both options at the time of filing, this behavior indicates that the value they place on the familiar paper process and related non-monetary benefits is at least $50 per filing. DHS recognizes that not all requestors had this choice clearly presented or practically available at the time of filing, and their costs may not be fully reflected in this amount. Moreover, DHS cannot reliably estimate which requestors had the e-filing option and discount effectively presented to them and therefore cannot aggregate this cost across the affected population. Under this rule, requestors who would have preferred paper filing and cannot establish eligibility for a waiver of the e-filing requirement will no longer have the option to file a paper benefit request and will be required to file electronically, which will result in the loss. Therefore, DHS treats $50 as a conservative estimate of the per-filing cost imposed by mandatory electronic filing for those with a meaningful choice between paper and electronic filing.
转移支付
Transfers
DHS目前对符合条件的电子提交表格提供50美元的费用减免。尽管该折扣金额反映了电子申请为机构带来较低资源成本这一事实,但该费用调整本身属于转移支付,而非对社会的成本节约。该减免降低了申请人支付的费用,同时相应减少了美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)的费用收入。由于这一变化反映的是支付的再分配,而非经济资源总使用量的变化,DHS将这50美元的电子申请费用折扣视为政府向申请人的一项转移支付。表17显示,预计每年的转移支付总额约为1.4亿美元。有关强制电子申请为USCIS带来的成本节约和收益的详细讨论,见第V.B.4.c节“对联邦政府的成本、成本节约及收益”。
DHS currently provides a $50 fee reduction for eligible forms e-filed. Although the amount of the discount reflects the fact that e-filing results in lower agency resource costs, the fee adjustment itself represents a transfer rather than cost savings to society. The reduction decreases the payment made by requestors while correspondingly decreasing fee revenue to USCIS. Because this change reflects a redistribution of payment rather than a change in the total use of economic resources, DHS treats the $50 e-filing fee discount as a transfer from the government to requestors. Table 17 shows the estimated annual transfer of approximately $140 million. Cost savings and benefits to USCIS associated with mandating e-filing are discussed greater in detail in Section V.B.4.c. Costs, Cost Savings, and Benefits to the Federal Government.
(印刷页第51964页)
( printed page 51964)
b. 无法量化的影响
b. Unquantifiable Impact
本规则将“电子申请”定义为以USCIS提供并批准的任何方式,以电子形式提交福利申请、佐证材料、文件、通知及通信,包括通过在线填写表格、通过门户网站、电子界面完成提交,或通过已获批准的在线账户上传已填写完成表格的PDF文件。对“电子申请”一词进行定义和使用预计不会产生经济影响,但将使本规则以及未来的规则更加清晰,并通过避免重复使用多个词语来表达相同意图,从而简化网站、指南和通信文件的撰写工作。USCIS目前允许申请人通过引导式在线申请流程提交福利申请,或在https://my.uscis.gov网站上传已填写完成的福利申请PDF文件进行提交。DHS对“电子申请”一词的定义旨在涵盖目前可用的各种电子申请方式,并保持足够的灵活性,以适应USCIS未来可能开发的任何新型或其他类型的电子申请方式。
This rule defines “e-file” to mean submission of a benefit request, supporting evidence, documents, notices, and communication electronically in any manner made available and approved by USCIS, including by completing the form online, in a web portal, electronic interface, or by uploading a PDF of the completed form through an approved online account. Defining and using the term “e-file” is not expected to have an economic impact, but it will make this rule, as well as future rules, clearer and will simplify drafting for websites, guidance, and communications by avoiding the repetitive use of multiple words to describe our intent. USCIS currently allows requestors to submit a benefit request through a guided online filing experience or by uploading a PDF of the benefit request online at https://my.uscis.gov. DHS defines e-file to account for the electronic filing methods currently available and to remain flexible enough to account for any future or additional types of e-filing USCIS may develop.
电子申请将通过降低因可预防的错误而导致裁决延误的可能性,减轻申请人的负担。这进而可以减少因等待获得工作许可、旅行许可或身份变更而产生的时间和间接成本。此外,电子申请还降低了申请人因纸质申请被拒收或提交不完整而错过截止日期,从而丧失移民福利资格的风险。
E-filing will reduce the burden on requestors by lowering the likelihood of adjudication delays caused by preventable errors. This, in turn, can reduce the time and indirect costs associated with waiting to work, travel, or change status. In addition, e-filing decreases the risk that requestors will lose eligibility for an immigration benefit because of missed deadlines stemming from rejected or incomplete paper submissions.
电子申请还将为申请人提供非货币性利益。近乎实时的提交、确认及状态更新,使申请人能够更快获得反馈,并更清楚地了解案件进展情况。此外,引导式在线申请,包括提示性的证据类别和实时错误检查,使申请人更容易在首次尝试时正确完成申请。
E-filing will also provide non-monetary benefits to requestors. Nearly real-time submission, confirmation, and status updates give requestors faster feedback and greater transparency into the progress of their cases. In addition, guided online filing, including prompted evidence categories and real-time error checks, makes it easier for requestors to correctly complete applications on the first attempt.
欺诈侦测和国家安全方面的改进亦为申请人和公众带来非金钱性利益。如后文详述,电子记录支持数据分析、模式侦测、欺诈分析以及审查和背景调查,从而加强欺诈预防和国家安全审查。更强的审查和分析工具有助于防止身份欺诈和申请人个人信息的滥用,降低他人欺诈行为延误或使合法案件复杂化的风险。更有效地侦测欺诈性(第51965页)申请可以减少整体系统积压,使善意申请能够得到更高效的处理。这些改进提高了移民系统的完整性和公平性,使申请人更加确信其案件会得到准确评估,且福利会授予符合条件的个人。
Improvements in fraud detection and national security also provide non-monetary benefits to requestors and the public. As explained more fully later, digital records support analytics, pattern detection, fraud analysis, and vetting and background checks to strengthen fraud prevention and national security screening. Stronger screening and analytic tools help prevent identity fraud and misuse of requestor personal information, lowering the risk that fraudulent activity of someone else will delay or complicate a legitimate case. More effective detection of fraudulent ( printed page 51965) filings can reduce overall system backlogs, allowing bona fide applications to be processed more efficiently. These enhancements increase the integrity and fairness of the immigration system, giving requestors greater confidence that their cases are evaluated accurately and that benefits are granted to eligible individuals.
对于部分申请人而言,强制电子申报可能带来一些难以量化的额外成本。尽管国土安全部(DHS)估计电子申报平均而言可减少申报时间负担和其他成本,但个人的实际体验可能因其具体情况、申报习惯以及对电子申报的熟悉程度而有所不同。过去数年中纸质申报持续被使用,这表明部分申请人可能认为电子申报存在某些成本或劣势,而这些成本或劣势并未能充分体现在定量成本之中。关于在线服务的研究表明,此类认知成本可能包括对新渠道相较于既有流程表现的不确定性,以及改变既定做法和学习新系统所需的时间和精力。[155]因此,国土安全部认识到,本分析中所列的货币化估算可能无法完全反映个别申请人所经历的全部成本。
There may be additional costs to some requestors associated with mandatory e-filing that are difficult to quantify. While DHS estimates that e-filing reduces filing time burden and other costs on average, individual experiences may differ depending on their circumstances, filing practices, and familiarity with e-filing. The continued use of paper filing over the past several years suggests that some requestors may perceive costs or disadvantages associated with e-filing that are not readily captured in the quantitative costs. Research on online services indicates that such perceived costs can include uncertainty about how a new channel will perform relative to an established process, and the time and effort required to change established practices and learn a new system.[155] DHS therefore recognizes that the monetized estimate presented in this analysis may not fully reflect all costs experienced by individual requestors.
一个潜在的不确定性来源在于,电子申报所估算的时间节省代表的是整个申报群体的平均效果。尽管国土安全部预计电子申报总体上会减少申报时间负担,但部分申请人可能无法体验到相同程度的减少。例如,已为准备纸质申报开发内部软件或建立既定工作流程的组织,其实现的时间节省可能小于估算值,因为它们已经在使纸质申报对其具体情况而言相对高效的流程上进行了投入。同样,个人体验也可能因申报的复杂程度、此前对纸质流程的投入程度,或对电子申报系统的熟悉程度而有所不同。
One potential source of uncertainty is that the estimated time savings from e-filing represent average effects across the filing population. Although DHS expects e-filing to reduce filing time burden overall, some requestors may not experience the same reduction. For example, organizations that have developed internal software or established workflows for preparing paper filings may realize smaller time savings than estimated, because they have already invested in processes that make paper filing relatively efficient for their specific circumstances. Similarly, individual experiences may vary depending on the complexity of the filing, the extent of prior investments in paper-based procedures, or familiarity with the e-filing system.
部分申请人在采用电子申报时也可能产生一次性的转换成本。这些成本可能包括学习新的申报流程、更新内部程序、培训员工,或修改现有软件或工作流程。这些转换成本可能包括与理解新要求相关的搜索和评估成本、与重新配置现有流程和工具相关的转移成本,以及与熟练掌握电子系统相关的学习成本。这些过渡成本的大小预计在不同申报人之间存在显著差异,且难以量化。对许多申请人而言,这些成本可能是暂时的,并会随时间推移被电子申报所带来的持续效率提升所抵消,而对另一些申请人而言,这些成本可能构成采用电子申报的更大障碍。
Some requestors may also incur one time switching costs associated with adopting e-filing. These costs may include learning a new filing process, updating internal procedures, training staff, or modifying existing software or workflows. These switching costs can include search and evaluation costs associated with understanding new requirements, transfer costs associated with reconfiguring existing processes and tools, and learning costs associated with gaining proficiency with the electronic system. The magnitude of these transition costs is expected to vary substantially across filers and is difficult to quantify. For many requestors, these costs may be temporary and offset over time by the recurring efficiencies associated with e-filing, while for others they may represent a more significant barrier to adoption.
此外,一些申请人指出了当前电子申请系统存在的实际局限,例如文件大小限制、相关表格无法一并提交的限制,以及对保留纸质申请材料包的偏好。部分申请人在组装和提交纸质申请材料时,尤其是对于涉及大量支持性文件的复杂案件,可能会感到更有信心或更具掌控感。关于电子报税采用情况的研究表明,感知风险和对电子服务提供方的信任是决定使用在线系统意愿的重要因素,这表明部分申请人可能需要付出额外的、未货币化的努力,以建立对电子提交的可靠性、完整性和安全性的信心。[156] 这些因素构成了从纸质申请过渡到电子申请过程中与转换相关的未货币化成本,即便在电子申请成为强制要求之前提供了最长达八个月的实施期也是如此。尽管美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)持续改进其电子申请系统,这些因素仍可能影响部分申请人的申请偏好,且难以进行货币化评估。
In addition, some requestors have identified practical limitations of the current e-filing system, such as file size restrictions, limitations on submitting related forms together, and a preference for maintaining paper filing packages. Some requestors may also perceive greater confidence or control when assembling and submitting paper filings, particularly for complex cases involving extensive supporting documentation. Research on electronic tax filing adoption indicates that perceived risk and trust in the electronic provider are important determinants of willingness to use online systems, suggesting that some requestors may incur additional, non-monetized effort to gain confidence in the reliability, completeness, and security of electronic submissions.[156] These factors represent non monetized switching costs associated with transitioning from paper to e-, even with up to eight months implementation period provided before e-filing becomes mandatory. Although USCIS continues to improve its e-filing system, these considerations may affect some requestors' filing preferences and are difficult to monetize.
美国国土安全部(DHS)认识到,这些因素可能对部分申请人造成本分析所列量化估算未能完全体现的成本。由于这些成本的性质和规模因申请人而异,且无法可靠地加以衡量,美国国土安全部未对其进行量化。美国国土安全部欢迎公众就强制电子申请相关的此类潜在转换成本及其他未量化成本的性质、规模和持续时间提出意见。
DHS recognizes that these factors may result in costs for some requestors that are not fully reflected in the quantified estimates presented in this analysis. Because the nature and magnitude of these costs vary across requestors and cannot be reliably measured, DHS has not quantified them. DHS welcomes public comment on the nature, magnitude, and duration of these potential switching costs and other unquantified costs associated with mandatory e-filing.
本规则还可能间接影响非营利组织、社区组织、法律服务提供者、难民安置机构、地方图书馆以及其他协助个人准备和提交移民福利申请的实体。由于不熟悉电子申请的个人可能寻求帮助创建在线账户、使用电子申请系统、上传支持性文件以及完成电子提交,这些组织可能面临对技术援助需求的增加。在此类协助系无偿提供的情况下,这些实体在向强制电子申请过渡期间可能面临更高的员工时间或资源需求。与此同时,电子申请可能减轻与纸质申请相关的行政负担,例如打印、复印、邮寄和保存纸质记录,从而随着时间推移提高运营效率。美国国土安全部请求公众就强制电子申请对协助申请人和请愿人准备或提交福利申请的这些组织可能产生的间接影响提出意见。
This rule may also indirectly affect nonprofit organizations, community-based organizations, legal service providers, refugee resettlement agencies, local libraries, and other entities that assist individuals in preparing and submitting immigration benefit requests. These organizations may see increased demand for technical assistance as individuals unfamiliar with electronic filing seek help creating online accounts, navigating the electronic filing system, uploading supporting documentation, and completing electronic submissions. Where such assistance is provided without additional compensation, these entities may face higher staff time or resource demands during the transition to mandatory e-filing. At the same time, e-filing may lessen administrative burdens associated with paper applications, such as printing, copying, mailing, and maintaining physical records, which could improve operational efficiency over time. DHS requests public comments on the potential indirect impacts of mandatory e-filing on these organizations that assist applicants and petitioners in preparing or submitting benefit requests.
c. 对联邦政府的成本、成本节约和效益
c. Costs, Cost Savings, and Benefits to the Federal Government
美国国土安全部预计,本规则将减少基于纸质的处理流程,并为美国公民及移民服务局带来成本节约和运营效率的提升。如前言所述,美国公民及移民服务局的锁箱(Lockbox)网络目前每年接收、开启、分类和扫描数百万份纸质福利申请及相关付款。这些活动耗费大量人力且耗时。在2025财政年度,美国公民及移民服务局在锁箱方面产生了395,991,902美元的费用。这些费用包括人工、设施、技术和邮资。在此总额中,253,244,458美元为与锁箱收件操作相关的人工成本。[157] 这些人工成本源于对纸质申请的人工收件处理(例如拆信、分类、扫描、录入数据、执行质量控制以及准备转运)。本规则生效后,如果美国公民及移民服务局能够通过电子申请减少或消除这些人工步骤,美国国土安全部估计,美国公民及移民服务局每年将可避免目前用于纸质收件处理的约2.2亿美元人工支出。[158]
DHS anticipates that this rule would result in a reduction in paper-based processing and generate cost savings and operational efficiencies for USCIS. As discussed in preamble, the USCIS Lockbox network currently receives, opens, sorts, and scans millions of paper benefit requests and associated payments each year. These activities are labor-intensive and time-consuming. In FY 2025, USCIS incurred $395,991,902 in lockbox costs. These costs include labor, facilities, technology, and postage. Of this total, $253,244,458 represents labor costs associated with lockbox intake operations.[157] These labor costs are attributable to the manual intake of paper filings ( e.g., extracting mail, sorting, scanning, keying data, performing quality control, and preparing shipments). When the rule is in effect, if USCIS can reduce or eliminate these manual steps through e-filing, DHS estimates that USCIS would avoid approximately $220 million per year in labor expenditures currently devoted to paper intake.[158]
纸质作业产生大量邮资和运输成本。在2025财政年度,美国公民及移民服务局仅邮资支出就达10,864,781美元,用于支持纸质处理工作。美国国土安全部估计,强制电子申请每年将使此类邮资成本减少约950万美元(印刷页第51966页)。[159] 这是美国公民及移民服务局因强制实施电子申请而实现的年度成本节约。
Paper-based operations impose substantial postage and shipping costs. In FY2025, USCIS spent $10,864,781 on postage alone to support paper-based processing. DHS estimates that mandatory e-filing would reduce these postage costs by approximately $9.5 ( printed page 51966) million annually.[159] This is annual cost savings to USCIS from mandating e-filing.
美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)预计强制电子申报将通过减少对纸质A类档案及存储和管理这些档案所需基础设施的依赖,从而产生成本节约。USCIS目前在142个设施中维护约5860万份现用档案,并与美国国家档案和记录管理局(NARA)或商业供应商共同保管5800万份已归档的A类档案,保管期限通常为归档后50年,每年处理超过80万次已归档档案的调取。随着越来越多的案件“天生”即为数字化案件,纸质档案的增长、档案调取以及相关的处理、运送、租赁和长期存储成本将会下降。电子申报还可避免未来因NARA要求而产生的转换成本,该要求规定自2022年12月31日起,所有移交的记录均须为电子形式。目前以电子方式提交的记录在归档时将无需再进行数字化处理。
USCIS expects mandatory e-filing to generate cost savings by reducing reliance on paper A-Files and the infrastructure needed to store and manage them. USCIS currently maintains about 58.6 million active files across 142 facilities and 58 million retired A-Files with NARA or commercial vendors, often for 50 years after retirement, and processes over 800,000 retired-file retrievals annually. As more cases are “born” digital, the growth of paper files, retrievals, and associated handling, shipping, leasing, and long-term storage costs will decline. E-filing also avoids future conversion costs triggered by NARA's requirement that, beginning December 31, 2022, all transferred records be electronic; records filed electronically now will not need later digitization at retirement.
电子申报预计还将通过减少因纸质档案丢失或放错位置而产生的成本,以及通过实物档案审计来降低成本。在2025财年,共报告86,384份档案丢失或遗失,这需要工作人员耗费时间寻找或重新制作这些档案,从而延误裁决,并增加基于不完整记录作出决定的风险。为核实USCIS及外部合作伙伴所持档案的位置而进行的实物档案审计,同样需要耗费大量人力进行人工检查。通过限制纸质档案的产生和流转,电子记录降低了档案丢失的风险和成本,并减少了对资源密集型审计的需求,从而提高整体效率并降低运营开支。
E-filing is further expected to reduce costs due to missing or misplaced paper files and by physical file audits. In FY 2025, 86,384 files were reported missing or lost, requiring staff time to locate or recreate them, delaying adjudications, and increasing the risk of decisions made on incomplete records. Physical file audits, conducted to verify the location of files held by USCIS and external partners, also require labor intensive, manual inspection. By limiting the creation and movement of paper files, electronic records reduce the risk and cost of lost files and lessen the need for resource intensive audits, improving overall efficiency and reducing operational expenses.
电子申报将通过降低可避免的返工量来降低USCIS的处理成本。因签名缺失、表格版本错误、费用金额错误及数据不完整而导致的驳回减少,将降低需要重复审查和处理的申请数量。此外,结构更完善的电子表格及实时验证预计将减少对证据补充请求(Requests for Evidence)和拒绝意向通知(Notices of Intent to Deny)的需求,从而减少起草、签发和处理此类通知所需的工作人员时间。
E-filing will reduce USCIS processing costs by lowering the volume of avoidable rework. Fewer rejections for missing signatures, incorrect form versions, incorrect fee amounts, and incomplete data will decrease the number of filings that must be reviewed and handled more than once. In addition, better-structured electronic forms and real-time validation are expected to reduce the need for Requests for Evidence and Notices of Intent to Deny, cutting the staff time required to draft, issue, and process such notices.
电子申报还将为USCIS带来运营效益。直接电子化受理提高了数据质量和标准化程度,从而支持更一致、及时和准确的裁决。此外,减少因可避免的申报错误而造成的处理延误,将有助于USCIS更好地管理积案并实现既定的服务目标。完全电子化的记录使USCIS能够在无需运送纸质档案的情况下实时在各办公室之间重新分配工作,并使多个办公室能够同时访问同一案件。这种灵活性有助于USCIS减少因局部工作量激增而造成的延误,并提高该机构利用现有人力管理案件量的能力,从而降低每个案件的运营成本。
E-filing will also provide operational benefits to USCIS. Direct electronic intake improves data quality and standardization, which supports more consistent, timely, and accurate adjudications. In addition, reducing processing delays caused by avoidable filing errors will help USCIS better manage case backlogs and meet established service goals. Fully electronic records allow USCIS to redistribute work across offices in real time without shipping paper files and to give multiple offices simultaneous access to the same case. This flexibility helps USCIS reduce delays caused by localized surges in workload and improves the agency's ability to manage caseloads with its existing workforce, lowering the operational cost per case.
欺诈侦测和国家安全方面的改善主要是USCIS和美国国土安全部(DHS)获得的非货币性效益。在以纸质为基础的流程下,欺诈侦测和国家安全审查受限于有限的、经选择性录入的数据。电子申报和完全数字化的记录能够以结构化数据形式捕获100%的信息,并支持跨案件和跨系统的文本分析、模式侦测、更快速和更广泛的欺诈趋势分析,以及持续审查和自动化背景调查。这些能力加强了欺诈的预防和侦测,提升了国家安全审查水平,并使欺诈侦测与国家安全局(FDNS)工作人员的时间得到更有效的利用。
Improvements in fraud detection and national security are primarily non-monetary benefits to USCIS and DHS. Under a paper-based process, fraud detection and national security vetting are constrained by limited, selectively keyed data. E-filing and fully digital records capture 100 percent of the information as structured data and support cross-case and cross-system text analytics, pattern detection, faster and broader fraud trend analysis, and continuous vetting and automated background checks. These capabilities strengthen fraud prevention and detection, enhance national security screening, and enable more effective use of FDNS staff time.
信息共享和诉讼支持的增强将为USCIS、DHS的其他部门、美国国务院及其他政府合作伙伴带来运营效益,并为法院和申请人带来间接效益。电子记录可在各机构之间快速共享,并可同时用于裁决、安全审查和诉讼,从而消除因纸质档案流转而产生的延误。这使得机构能够更快地应对安全事件,并更及时、高效地在诉讼及其他监督程序中提供行政记录。
Enhanced information sharing and litigation support will provide operational benefits to USCIS, other DHS components, the Department of State, and other government partners, with indirect benefits to courts and requestors. Electronic records can be rapidly shared across agencies and used simultaneously for adjudications, security reviews, and litigation, eliminating delays tied to moving paper files. This allows faster responses to security incidents and more timely, efficient production of administrative records in litigation and other oversight processes.
数据质量和完整性的改善是USCIS及依赖USCIS数据的合作机构所获得的效益。在线表格强制执行标准化的格式和定义,并消除了人工数据录入中产生的许多转录错误。因此,USCIS的数据变得更加准确、一致和完整,从而支持更好的分析、报告和决策支持,并使其更易于与其他使用或共享移民数据的系统实现互操作。
Improvements in data quality and integrity are benefits that accrue to USCIS and partner agencies that rely on USCIS data. Online forms enforce standardized formats and definitions and remove many transcription errors that arise from manual data entry. As a result, USCIS data become more accurate, consistent, and complete, supporting better analytics, reporting, and decision support, and making it easier to interoperate with other systems that consume or share immigration data.
用户体验和信息获取方面的改进对申请人和公众均有益处。集中化的myUSCIS账户为申请人提供对其申请历史、案件状态和通知的统一访问,以及实时更新和通讯。
User experience and access to information improvements are benefits for requestors and the public. Centralized myUSCIS accounts provide requestors with consolidated access to their filing history, case status, and notices, as well as real-time updates and communications.
安全与隐私方面的增强主要使申请人受益,同时也为美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)带来风险降低的好处。myUSCIS中的在线账户采用双重身份验证,相较于可能被误投递或在共用邮箱中被他人获取的实体邮件,为申请人提供了更可控、更私密的通知和个人信息访问方式。这种对敏感信息的更强保护,对于犯罪或虐待受害者等弱势群体尤为重要,因为他们的安全和福祉可能取决于其移民记录的保密性。
Security and privacy enhancements are benefits primarily for requestors, with risk reduction benefits for USCIS. Online accounts in myUSCIS use two factor authentication and give requestors more controlled and private access to their notices and personal information than physical mail, which may be misdelivered or accessible in shared mailboxes. This stronger protection of sensitive information is especially important for vulnerable populations, such as victims of crime or abuse, whose safety and wellbeing may depend on the confidentiality of their immigration records.
强制实行电子申报要求美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)承担与信息技术相关的资源成本。这些成本来自于为接收、存储、处理和裁决电子提交材料所需的系统运行和支持。美国国土安全部(DHS)预计将产生与确保符合无障碍标准、系统测试以及制定应急计划(以减轻潜在的服务中断或系统停机)相关的成本。DHS还预计,与在线账户访问、文件上传和系统导航相关的使用协助需求将增加,这将需要技术支持资源。本规则确立了申请豁免电子申报要求的程序,这将产生审查豁免申请、处理相关文件以及裁决与豁免表格相关的费用豁免申请所需的行政成本。
Mandating e-filing requires USCIS to incur information technology related resource costs. These costs arise from the operation and support of systems necessary to receive, store, process, and adjudicate electronic submissions. DHS expects costs associated with ensuring compliance with accessibility standards, system testing, and contingency planning to mitigate potential service disruptions or system downtime. DHS also anticipates increased demand for use assistance related to online account access, document uploads, and system navigation requiring technical support resources. This rule establishes a process for requesting a waiver from the e-filing requirement, which will incur administrative costs to review exemption requests, process associated documentation and adjudicate fee waiver submissions related to the exemption form.
《移民与国籍法》(INA)规定,应按足以确保全额收回DHS提供裁决和归化服务成本的水平征收费用,包括行政成本以及向某些申请人和请愿人免费提供的服务。INA第286(m)条,8 U.S.C. 1356(m)。DHS通常根据相对裁决负担和USCIS资源使用情况估算的裁决成本来制定USCIS费用。[160] 费用的设定水平应足以收回这些指定成本,例如文员、官员和管理人员的薪酬及福利,外加一笔用于收回未分配间接费用和免费提供的移民福利成本的金额。
The INA provides for the collection of fees at a level that will ensure recovery of the full costs of providing adjudication and naturalization services by DHS, including administrative costs and services provided without charge to certain applicants and petitioners. INA sec. 286(m), 8 U.S.C. 1356(m). DHS generally establishes USCIS fees according to the estimated cost of adjudication based on its relative adjudication burden and use of USCIS resources.[160] Fees are established at an amount that is necessary to recover these assigned costs, such as clerical, officer, and managerial salaries and benefits, plus an amount to recover unassigned overhead and immigration benefits provided without a fee charge.
DHS在2024财年费用规则中根据实证成本估算确定了其大多数表格当前的费用。随着时间推移,与电子申报增加相关的运营效率提升可能会降低USCIS的裁决和受理成本。只要这些节省得以持续,它们将被纳入(印刷页51967)未来的费用审查中,这可能会降低机构的总体成本收回要求,并相对于纸质操作模式减缓未来费用水平的增长。
DHS established the current fee for most of its forms in FY 2024 fee rule based on empirical cost estimates. Over time, the operational efficiencies associated with increased e-filing may reduce USCIS adjudication and intake costs. To the extent these savings are sustained, they will be incorporated into ( printed page 51967) future fee reviews, potentially reducing the agency's overall cost recovery requirement and slowing the growth in the future fee levels relative to a paper-based operation.
强制实行电子申报可能对地方社区机构,特别是公共图书馆产生间接影响,因为图书馆通常是缺乏可靠互联网服务或计算资源的个人获取服务的途径。随着申请人从纸质提交转向在线提交移民福利申请,图书馆可能会经历对公共电脑、互联网带宽以及打印或扫描服务需求的增加,同时也需要投入更多工作人员时间协助使用者进行基本的数字化操作和文件准备。这些影响代表着资源利用从移民福利申请人向地方资助机构的转移,并可能对数字服务匮乏人群或移民人群比例较高的社区产生不成比例的影响。尽管由于数据限制难以量化这些影响,但预计不会很显著。
Mandating e-filing may generate indirect impacts on local community institutions, particularly public libraries, which often serve as access points for individuals who lack reliable internet service or computing resources. As requestors shift from paper-based to online submission of immigration benefit requests, libraries may experience increased demand for public computers, internet bandwidth, and printing or scanning services, as well as additional staff time devoted to assisting patrons with basic digital navigation and document preparation. These effects represent a shift in resource utilization from immigration benefit requestors to locally funded institutions and may disproportionately affect communities with higher concentrations of digitally underserved or immigrant populations. Although these impacts are difficult to quantify due to data limitations, they are not expected to be substantial.
5. 已考虑的替代方案
5. Alternatives Considered
根据本规则,如果某一福利申请已可供电子提交至少180天,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)即可要求对该申请实行电子提交。国土安全部(DHS)还考虑了一种替代方案,即仅在某一表格的自愿电子提交率达到特定门槛后才启动强制电子提交。国土安全部认定,将强制要求与电子提交采用率门槛挂钩,不如采用附带提前通知的基于时间的门槛更为适当。采用使用率门槛的做法将使申请人难以预测强制电子提交的生效日期,因为其时间取决于整体使用率何时恰好超过特定百分比。这种不确定性将使美国公民及移民服务局更难规划内部资源和内部系统变更。事先确定适当的门槛存在困难:门槛过低可能无法充分保证可用性和可及性,而门槛过高则可能对某些表格或特定群体而言不切实际。此外,某些服务于采用行为较为保守的特定群体的表格可能永远无法达到规定门槛,这实际上将使美国公民及移民服务局无限期地被迫同时维持纸质和电子两套流程,即便在电子提交具有操作可行性且符合公共利益的情况下亦是如此。
Under this rule, USCIS may require e-filing for any benefit request that has been available for e-filing for at least 180 days. DHS also considered an alternative under which mandatory e-filing would begin only after a form reached a specified threshold of voluntary e-filings. DHS determined that tying a mandate to an e-filing adoption threshold is less appropriate than using a time-based threshold with advance notice. A usage threshold approach would make the effective date of mandatory e-filing unpredictable for requestors because the timing would depend on when overall usage happens to cross a specified percentage. This uncertainty would make it more difficult for USCIS to plan internal resources and internal system changes. It is difficult to determine an appropriate threshold in advance: a threshold that is too low may provide little assurance about usability and access, while a higher threshold could be unrealistic for certain forms or populations. In addition, some forms serving specific populations with more reserved adoption behavior might never reach the specified threshold, effectively locking USCIS into maintaining dual paper and electronic processes indefinitely, even where e-filing is operationally viable and in the public interest.
然而,本规则采用的做法,即仅允许美国公民及移民服务局在某一表格已可电子提交至少180天后才强制要求电子提交,并在生效日期前提供附带60天宽限期的提前公众通知,这为美国公民及移民服务局提供了对该流程更大的掌控力。该做法为申请人提供了明确的最短适应期,以适应电子提交的可用性,同时保留了美国公民及移民服务局在强制要求电子提交之前考虑系统性能和其他操作因素的灵活性。如果受影响群体在强制要求实施后完全停止提交相关申请,该强制要求亦可予以撤销。
However, the approach in this rule—allowing USCIS to mandate e-filing only after a form has been available electronically for at least 180 days and providing advance public notice with a 60-day grace period before the effective date—offers USCIS more control over the process. It gives requestors a defined minimum period to adjust to the availability of e-filing while preserving flexibility for USCIS to consider system performance and other operational factors before making e-filing mandatory. The mandate is also reversible should the affected population stop filing the applicable request altogether following the mandate.
国土安全部考虑了但未采纳若干关于电子提交要求豁免的替代方案,例如允许申请人将豁免申请与纸质福利申请一并提交、允许提交豁免申请而无需使用标准化表格,或提供免费豁免。国土安全部最终认定,这些替代方案均不优于其所选定的做法,即要求在新设立的标准化表格上单独预先提交豁免申请并缴纳费用。
DHS considered, but did not adopt, several alternative approaches to the waiver of e-filing requirement, such as allowing requestors to submit a waiver request together with the paper benefit request, allowing waiver requests without a standardized form, or providing waivers without a fee. DHS ultimately determined these alternatives are not superior to the chosen approach of requiring a separate, pre-filed waiver on a standardized newly created form with a fee.
允许申请人将豁免申请与纸质福利申请一并提交,将损害豁免程序的主要目的之一,即让美国公民及移民服务局提前掌握纸质提交情况,以便规划其对电子受理工作造成的影响。如果豁免申请与纸质福利申请一并送达,美国公民及移民服务局仍需在接受该申请之前先行审查豁免申请,这可能提高拒收率,并使申请人对其提交是否会被视为妥善收悉产生不确定性。将大批申请材料邮寄退回申请人,将削减本规则带来的邮寄成本节省。相比之下,要求在提交纸质申请之前先行获得豁免批准,可为申请人提供更明确的预期,并使美国公民及移民服务局能够更准确地预测和管理纸质案件的数量。
Allowing requestors to submit a waiver request together with the paper benefit request would undermine one of the main purposes of the waiver process to give USCIS advance visibility into paper filing so that it can plan the disruption to electronic intake. If waiver requests arrived bundled with paper benefit requests, USCIS would still need to review the waiver before accepting the filing, which could increase rejection rates and create uncertainty for requestors about whether their filings would be treated as properly received. Mailing large request packages back to the requester would reduce themail savings from this rule. By contrast, requiring a pre-approved waiver before a paper filing is submitted provides clearer expectations for requestors and allows USCIS to more accurately anticipate and manage the volume of paper cases.
同样,不采用标准化豁免表格将降低该流程的效率和一致性。专用表格可确保美国公民及移民服务局以统一方式收集评估困难主张所需的具体信息,支持更一致的裁决,并便于随时间追踪豁免数量和结果,以用于项目管理和未来的费用审查。非正式或无固定格式的豁免申请将增加裁决所需时间,使标准的统一适用更加困难,并使数据收集和监督工作复杂化。
Similarly, not using a standardized waiver form would reduce the efficiency and consistency of the process. A dedicated form ensures that USCIS collects the specific information needed to evaluate hardship claims in a uniform manner, supports more consistent adjudication, and facilitates tracking of waiver volumes and outcomes over time for program management and future fee reviews. Informal or unstructured waiver requests would increase adjudication time, make it more difficult to apply standards consistently, and complicate data collection and oversight.
最后,尽管国土安全部(DHS)曾考虑提供免费豁免,但不收取费用的做法会将处理豁免请求的全部成本转嫁给其他缴费请求人,且无法遏制轻率或投机性的豁免申请。25美元的费用旨在收回与豁免请求受理相关的部分成本,这与国土安全部的成本回收权限相符,同时仍允许根据现行法规对指定类别给予费用豁免。国土安全部认定,相较于让其他请求人承担豁免程序的全部成本并避免不必要的豁免申请,这种做法能更好地平衡那些确实无法遵守电子申请要求者获得纸质申请选项的需求。
Finally, although DHS considered providing waivers without a fee, a no-fee approach would shift the full cost of processing waiver requests onto other fee-paying requestors and would not discourage frivolous or speculative waiver filings. The $25 fee is designed to recover at least part of the costs associated with intake of waiver requests, consistent with DHS's cost-recovery authority, while still allowing fee waivers for specified categories under existing regulations. DHS concluded that this approach better balances access to a paper-filing option for those who genuinely cannot comply with the e-filing requirement against the need to avoid imposing the full cost of the waiver process on other requestors and to discourage unnecessary waiver submissions.
6. 量化成本、收益和转移支付总额
6. Total Quantified Costs, Benefits, and Transfers
国土安全部估计,请求人将获得约5.33亿美元的量化成本节省总额。这些节省源于不再提交纸质福利请求、避免重新提交被驳回的纸质表格的必要性,以及减少与纸质申请相关的时间负担。国土安全部还估计,请求人将因电子申请要求豁免表格产生约1500万美元的新成本,包括完成该表格所需时间的机会成本和邮寄费用。仅就量化影响而言,净计算后,本规则预计将为请求人带来约5.18亿美元的成本节省。
DHS estimates that requestors will experience total quantified cost savings of approximately $533 million. These savings result from no longer filing paper-based benefit requests, avoiding the need to re-file rejected paper forms, and reducing the time burden associated with paper filing. DHS also estimates that requestors will incur about $15 million in new costs related to the waiver of e-filing requirement form, including the opportunity cost of the time needed to complete the form and mailing expenses. On net, based on quantified impacts only, the rule is expected to generate approximately $518 million in cost savings to requestors.
除这些成本影响外,本规则还产生了政府与请求人之间的转移支付。国土安全部估计,电子申请费折扣将使约1.4亿美元从政府转移给请求人。表18总结了本规则的年度量化经济影响。
In addition to these cost impacts, the rule generates transfers between the government and requestors. DHS estimates that an e-filing fee discount will transfer about $140 million from the government to requestors. Table 18 summarizes the annual quantified economic impact of the rule.
(印刷页51968)
( printed page 51968)
表19显示,在2027财年至2036财年的实施期间,国土安全部估计未折现净成本节省总额约为51.81亿美元,未折现净转移支付总额约为14亿美元(从政府转移给请求人)。按3%的贴现率折现后,10年期净成本节省约为44.20亿美元,转移支付约为11.94亿美元。按7%的贴现率折现后,10年期净成本节省约为36.39亿美元,净转移支付约为9.83亿美元。这些总额相当于在3%和7%两种贴现率下,年化净成本节省约为5.18亿美元,年化转移支付约为1.4亿美元。
Over the FY 2027-FY 2036 implementation period, Table 19 shows that DHS estimates total undiscounted net cost savings of about $5,181 million and undiscounted net transfers of about $1,400 million (from the government to requestors). When discounted at a 3-percent rate, the 10-year net cost savings are approximately $4,420 million and transfers are approximately $1,194 million. When discounted at a 7-percent rate, the 10-year net cost savings are approximately $3,639 million, and net transfers are approximately $983 million. These totals are equivalent to annualized net cost savings of about $518 million and annualized transfers of about $140 million at both the 3- and 7-percent discount rates.
C. 《管制灵活性法》(认证)
C. Regulatory Flexibility Act (Certification)
经《1996年小企业管制执行公平法》修订的《1980年管制灵活性法》(RFA)要求联邦机构在制定法规过程中考虑法规对小企业、小型政府辖区和小型组织的潜在影响。“小实体”一词包括小企业、独立拥有和经营且在其所属领域不占主导地位的非营利组织,以及人口少于50,000人的政府辖区。[161] “个人”不被视为小实体,规则对个人造成的成本不在《管制灵活性法》的考量范围之内。
The Regulatory Flexibility Act of 1980 (RFA), as amended by the Small Business Regulatory Enforcement Fairness Act of 1996, requires Federal agencies to consider the potential impact of regulations on small businesses, small governmental jurisdictions, and small organizations during the development of their rules. The term “small entities” comprises small businesses, not-for-profit organizations that are independently owned and operated and are not dominant in their fields, and governmental jurisdictions with populations of less than 50,000.[161] An “individual” is not considered a small entity and costs to an individual from a rule are not considered for RFA purposes.
《管制灵活性法》的管制灵活性分析要求仅适用于机构依据5 U.S.C. 553或任何其他法律必须发布拟议规则制定总通知的规则。见5 U.S.C. 604(a)。国土安全部未就本行动发布拟议规则制定通知。因此,国土安全部既无需证明本临时最终规则(IFR)不会对大量小实体产生重大经济影响,也无需进行管制灵活性分析。
The RFA's regulatory flexibility analysis requirements apply only to those rules for which an agency is required to publish a general notice of ( printed page 51969) proposed rulemaking pursuant to 5 U.S.C. 553 or any other law. See5 U.S.C. 604(a). DHS did not issue a notice of proposed rulemaking for this action. Accordingly, DHS is not required to either certify that this IFR would not have a significant economic impact on a substantial number of small entities nor conduct a regulatory flexibility analysis.
D. 《1995年无资金授权改革法》
D. Unfunded Mandates Reform Act of 1995
《1995年无资金授权改革法》(UMRA)的目的之一是遏制向州、地方和部落政府施加无资金联邦授权的做法。[162] UMRA第二章要求各联邦机构就最终规则中任何可能导致州、地方和部落政府合计或私营部门在任何一年内支出1亿美元或以上(每年根据通货膨胀进行调整)的联邦授权的影响编写书面声明。见2 U.S.C. 1532(a)。根据消费者价格指数(全体城市消费者,CPI-U),经通货膨胀调整后,1995年的1亿美元相当于2024年的约2.06亿美元。[163]
The Unfunded Mandates Reform Act of 1995 (UMRA) is intended, among other things, to curb the practice of imposing unfunded Federal mandates on State, local, and Tribal governments.[162] Title II of UMRA requires each Federal agency to prepare a written statement assessing the effects of any Federal mandate in a final rule that includes any Federal mandate that may result in a $100 million or more expenditure (adjusted annually for inflation) in any one year by State, local, and Tribal governments, in the aggregate, or by the private sector. See2 U.S.C. 1532(a). The inflation adjusted value of $100 million in 1995 is approximately $206 million in 2024 based on the Consumer Price Index for All Urban Consumers (CPI-U).[163]
“联邦强制令”一词是指联邦政府间强制令或联邦私营部门强制令。参见《美国法典》第2编第1502(1)条、第658(6)条。“联邦政府间强制令”一词在相关方面是指将对州、地方或部落政府施加可强制执行义务的条款(作为联邦援助条件或因参与自愿性联邦计划而产生的义务除外)。《美国法典》第2编第658(5)条。“联邦私营部门强制令”一词在相关方面是指将对私营部门施加可强制执行义务的条款(作为联邦援助条件或因参与自愿性联邦计划而产生的义务除外)。参见《美国法典》第2编第658(7)条。
The term “Federal mandate” means a Federal intergovernmental mandate or a Federal private sector mandate. See2 U.S.C. 1502(1), 658(6). The term “Federal intergovernmental mandate” means, in relevant part, a provision that would impose an enforceable duty upon State, local, or Tribal governments (except as a condition of Federal assistance or a duty arising from participation in a voluntary Federal program). 2 U.S.C. 658(5). The term “Federal private sector mandate” means, in relevant part, a provision that would impose an enforceable duty upon the private sector except (except as a condition of Federal assistance or a duty arising from participation in a voluntary Federal program). See2 U.S.C. 658(7).
本规则不包含《无资金拨款强制令改革法》(UMRA)所定义的联邦强制令,因为本规则不对任何其他级别的政府或私营部门实体施加任何可强制执行的义务。此类实体所受的任何下游影响,均仅因其自愿选择而产生,而非可强制执行义务的后果。同样,对州和地方政府造成的任何成本或转移影响,也不是UMRA所定义的联邦强制令所致。参见《美国法典》第2编第1502(1)条、第658(6)条。因此,UMRA第二编的要求不适用于本规则,国土安全部(DHS)未依据UMRA编制相关声明。
This rule does not contain a Federal mandate as the term is defined under UMRA because it does not impose any enforceable duty upon any other level of government or private sector entity. Any downstream effects on such entities would arise solely due to their voluntary choices and would not be a consequence of an enforceable duty. Similarly, any costs or transfer effects on State and local governments would not result from a Federal mandate as that term is defined under UMRA. See2 U.S.C. 1502(1), 658(6). The requirements of title II of UMRA, therefore, do not apply, and DHS has not prepared a statement under UMRA.
E. 1996年《小企业管理公平执法法》(国会审查法)
E. Small Business Regulatory Enforcement Fairness Act of 1996 (Congressional Review Act)
国会审查法(CRA)作为1996年《小企业管理公平执法法》(SBREFA)的一部分,被纳入该法第E分章,公法第104-121号,第二编,《美国法规大全》第110卷第847页,第868页及以下(1996年3月29日)。本临时最终规则(IFR)符合《美国法典》第5编第804(2)条规定的标准,因为其可能对经济产生每年1亿美元或以上的影响。参见《美国法典》第5编第804(2)(A)条。国土安全部已遵守CRA的报告要求,并已按照《美国法典》第5编第801(a)(1)条的规定将本规则提交国会和审计长。如本序言所述,国土安全部认定有正当理由使本规则自公布之日起立即生效,因为无需履行通知和评论程序。《美国法典》第5编第808(2)条。
The Congressional Review Act (CRA) was included as part of the Small Business Regulatory Enforcement Fairness Act of 1996 (SBREFA) by subtitle E of SBREFA, Public Law 104-121, title II, 110 Stat. 847, 868, et seq. (Mar. 29, 1996). This IFR meets the criteria set forth in 5 U.S.C. 804(2) because it is likely to result in an annual effect on the economy of $100 million or more. See5 U.S.C. 804(2)(A). DHS has complied with the CRA's reporting requirements and has sent this rule to Congress and to the Comptroller General as required by 5 U.S.C. 801(a)(1). As stated in this preamble, DHS has found that there is good cause to make this rule effective immediately upon publication because notice and comment is not required. 5 U.S.C. 808(2).
F. 第13132号行政命令(联邦制)
F. Executive Order 13132 (Federalism)
本临时最终规则不会对各州、国家政府与各州之间的关系,或各级政府之间权力与职责的分配产生实质性直接影响。因此,根据第13132号行政命令第6节的规定,确定本规则不具有足以要求编制联邦制影响摘要声明的联邦制影响。
This IFR would not have substantial direct effects on the States, on the relationship between the National Government and the States, or on the distribution of power and responsibilities among the various levels of government. Therefore, in accordance with section 6 of E.O. 13132, it is determined that this rule does not have sufficient federalism implications to warrant the preparation of a federalism summary impact statement.
G. 第12988号行政命令(民事司法改革)
G. Executive Order 12988 (Civil Justice Reform)
本临时最终规则系依照第12988号行政命令《民事司法改革》起草和审查。本临时最终规则旨在为受影响行为提供明确的法律标准,并经过仔细审查以消除起草错误和歧义,从而最大限度减少诉讼并减轻联邦法院系统的不当负担。国土安全部已确定本规则符合第12988号行政命令第3节规定的适用标准。
This IFR is drafted and reviewed in accordance with E.O. 12988, Civil Justice Reform. This IFR was written to provide a clear legal standard for affected conduct and was reviewed carefully to eliminate drafting errors and ambiguities so as to minimize litigation and undue burden on the Federal Court system. DHS has determined that this rule meets the applicable standards provided in section 3 of E.O. 12988.
H. 家庭评估
H. Family Assessment
国土安全部已依照1999年《财政部总拨款法》第654节的要求对本规则进行了审查。参见公法第105-277号,《美国法规大全》第112卷第2681页(1998年)。国土安全部已系统审查第654(c)(1)节所规定的各项标准,评估本监管行动是否:(1)影响家庭的稳定性或安全性,特别是在婚姻承诺方面;(2)影响父母在子女教育、抚养和监督方面的权力;(3)有助于家庭履行其职能;(4)影响家庭和儿童的可支配收入或贫困状况;(5)仅在有正当理由的情况下对家庭产生财务影响(如有);(6)可由州或地方政府或家庭自行实施;或(7)就青少年行为与个人责任同社会规范之间的关系确立政策。如该机构认定某项法规可能对家庭福祉产生负面影响,则该机构必须就其实施提供充分的理由说明。
DHS has reviewed this rule in line with the requirements of section 654 of the Treasury General Appropriations Act, 1999. SeePublic Law 105-277, 112 Stat. 2681 (1998). DHS has systematically reviewed the criteria specified in section 654(c)(1), by evaluating whether this regulatory action: (1) impacts the stability or safety of the family, particularly in terms of marital commitment; (2) impacts the authority of parents in the education, nurture, and supervision of their children; (3) helps the family perform its functions; (4) affects disposable income or poverty of families and children; (5) only financially impacts families, if at all, to the extent such impacts are justified; (6) may be carried out by State or local government or by the family; or (7) establishes a policy concerning the relationship between the behavior and personal responsibility of youth and the norms of society. If the agency determines a regulation may negatively affect family well-being, then the agency must provide an adequate rationale for its implementation.
DHS没有数据表明本临时最终规则将对可支配收入或某些家庭和儿童(包括美国公民儿童)的贫困状况产生任何影响。DHS承认,本规则将对某些家庭施加一项新的、小额的必须缴纳的费用。然而,本临时最终规则将为USCIS和联邦政府提供资金,用于管理受影响的项目并实现本规则的意图。然而,25美元的费用对受影响家庭的可支配收入影响甚微。DHS还确定,本规则不会对家庭作为一种制度的自主性或完整性产生任何影响。
DHS has no data that indicate that this IFR will have any impacts on disposable income or the poverty of certain families and children, including U.S. citizen children. DHS acknowledges that this rule would impose a new, small fee that some families must submit. However, the IFR would provide USCIS and the Federal Government with funds that would be used to administer the affected programs and meet the rule's intent. However, a fee of $25 would have minute effect on the disposable income for the affected families. DHS also determined that this rule would not have any impact on the autonomy or integrity of the family as an institution.
I. 第13175号行政命令(与印第安部落政府的磋商与协调)
I. Executive Order 13175 (Consultation and Coordination With Indian Tribal Governments)
根据关于与印第安部落政府磋商与协调的第13175号行政命令,本临时最终规则不涉及部落事宜,因为它不会对一个或多个印第安部落、联邦政府与印第安部落之间的关系、或联邦政府与印第安部落之间权力与责任的分配产生实质性直接影响。(印刷页第51970页)
This IFR does not have Tribal implications under E.O. 13175, Consultation and Coordination with Indian Tribal Governments, because it will not have a substantial direct effect on one or more Indian Tribes, on the relationship between the Federal Government and Indian Tribes, or on the distribution of power and responsibilities between the Federal Government and Indian Tribes. ( printed page 51970)
J. 国家环境政策法(NEPA)
J. National Environmental Policy Act (NEPA)
国土安全部(DHS)及其下属机构对拟议的监管行动进行分析,以确定《国家环境政策法》(NEPA,42 U.S.C. 4321及以下条款)是否适用,如适用,则需确定所需分析的程度。DHS第023-01号指令第01次修订版《实施国家环境政策法》及第023-01-001-01号指导手册第01次修订版(“指导手册”)[164]确立了DHS及其下属机构用于遵守NEPA的政策和程序。
DHS and its components analyze proposed regulatory actions to determine whether the National Environmental Policy Act (NEPA), 42 U.S.C. 4321 et seq., applies and, if so, what degree of analysis is required. DHS Directive 023-01, Revision 01 “Implementing the National Environmental Policy Act” and Instruction Manual 023-01-001-01, Revision 01 (“Instruction Manual”) [164] establish the policies and procedures that DHS and its components use to comply with NEPA.
NEPA允许联邦机构在其NEPA实施程序中确立若干类别的行动(“类别排除”),根据经验表明,这些行动无论是单独还是累积,均不会对人类环境产生重大影响,因此不需要进行环境评估或环境影响声明。参见42 U.S.C. 4336(a)(2)和4336(e)(1)。《指导手册》附录A列出了DHS的类别排除事项。[165]
NEPA allows Federal agencies to establish, in their NEPA implementing procedures, categories of actions (“categorical exclusions”) that experience has shown do not, individually or cumulatively, have a significant effect on the human environment and, therefore, do not require an environmental assessment or environmental impact statement. See42 U.S.C. 4336(a)(2) and 4336(e)(1). The Instruction Manual, Appendix A lists the DHS Categorical Exclusions.[165]
根据DHS的NEPA实施程序,一项行动要获得类别排除,必须同时满足以下三个条件:(1)整个行动明确属于一项或多项类别排除范围;(2)该行动不是更大行动的一部分;以及(3)不存在可能产生重大环境影响的特殊情况。[166]
Under DHS NEPA implementing procedures, for an action to be categorically excluded, it must satisfy each of the following three conditions: (1) The entire action clearly fits within one or more of the categorical exclusions; (2) the action is not a piece of a larger action; and (3) no extraordinary circumstances exist that create the potential for a significant environmental effect.[166]
本临时最终规则仅限于修订DHS法规以扩大USCIS表格的电子提交使用范围。本规则纯属行政性和程序性规定。DHS已对本规则进行审查,认定本规则中的修订不会对环境造成重大影响,也不会导致任何环境影响方面的变化。
This interim final rule is limited to amending the DHS regulations to expand the use of e-filing for USCIS forms. This rule is strictly administrative and procedural. DHS has reviewed this rule and finds that no significant impact on the environment, or any change in environmental effect will result from the amendments in this rule.
因此,DHS认定,本规则对现行法规的修订明确符合DHS在其NEPA实施程序中确立的类别排除A3,属于不改变环境影响的行政性变更,不属于更大联邦行动的一部分,也不存在可能产生重大环境影响的特殊情况。因此,本规则所作的监管修订被归类排除,无需进一步进行NEPA审查。
Accordingly, DHS finds that the promulgation of this rule's amendments to current regulations clearly fits within categorical exclusion A3 established in DHS's NEPA implementing procedures as an administrative change with no change in environmental effect, is not part of a larger Federal action, and does not present extraordinary circumstances that create the potential for a significant environmental effect. Therefore, the regulatory amendments made in this rule are categorically excluded from further NEPA review.
K. 文书削减法(PRA)
K. Paperwork Reduction Act (PRA)
根据1995年《文书削减法》(公法104-13号,1995年5月22日),所有机构均须将规则中所包含的任何报告要求提交管理和预算办公室(OMB)审查和批准。本规则包含一项拟议的新信息收集事项,该事项将在意见征询期结束并对意见作出回应后提交OMB。虽然本规则所编纂的法规将按照上文“生效日期”部分所规定的日期生效,但本规则规定,强制要求以电子方式提交某一表格须以经批准的信息收集为前提,该信息收集可用于申请豁免电子提交。因此,在USCIS处理完就本规则所产生的信息收集在60天意见征询期内收到的意见、按照5 CFR 1320.12(d)的要求发布30天联邦公报通知,且OMB按照PRA要求对该信息收集请求作出结论之前,USCIS不会要求任何表格仅以电子方式提交。[167]
Under the Paperwork Reduction Act of 1995, Public Law 104-13 (May 22, 1995), all agencies are required to submit to OMB, for review and approval, any reporting requirements inherent in a rule. This rule contains a proposed new collection of information that will be submitted to OMB upon completion of the comment period and response to comments. While the regulations codified in this rule will be effective as provided in the DATES section above, this rule provides that mandating electronic submission of a certain form entails an approved information collection that can be used to request a waiver from e-filing. Accordingly, USCIS will not require that any form be submitted only electronically until USCIS addresses the comments received during the 60-day comment period on the information collection that this rule creates, publishes a 30-day Federal Register Notice as required under 5 CFR 1320.12(d), and OMB concludes the collection request as required by the PRA.[167]
美国国土安全部(DHS)和美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)邀请社会公众和其他联邦机构就拟议信息收集的影响提交意见。根据《文书削减法》(PRA)的规定,本规则在《联邦公报》上的公布即满足就新信息收集工具征求意见的义务。
DHS and USCIS invite the general public and other Federal agencies to comment on the impact to the proposed collection of information. In accordance with the PRA, publication of this rule in the Federal Register satisfies the obligation to obtain comments regarding the new information collection instrument.
我们鼓励公众提交意见,并将在本临时最终规则公布之日起60天内接受意见。所有提交材料均须在信函正文中注明OMB管理与预算办公室控制编号1615-NEW以及机构名称。请按照本规则“地址”和“一、公众参与”部分所述说明提交意见。有关本信息收集的意见应就以下四点中的一点或多点予以说明:
Comments are encouraged and will be accepted for 60 days from the publication date of this interim final rule. All submissions received must include the OMB Control Number 1615-NEW in the body of the letter and the agency name. Please follow the instructions as described under the ADDRESSES and I. Public Participation sections of this rule to submit comments. Comments on this information collection should address one or more of the following four points:
(1)评估该信息收集对于机构履行职能是否必要,包括该信息是否具有实际效用;
(1) Evaluate whether the collection of information is necessary for the proper performance of the functions of the agency, including whether the information will have practical utility;
(2)评估机构对信息收集负担估算的准确性,包括所用方法和假设的有效性;
(2) Evaluate the accuracy of the agency's estimate of the burden of the collection of information, including the validity of the methodology and assumptions used;
(3)提高拟收集信息的质量、效用和清晰度;以及
(3) Enhance the quality, utility, and clarity of the information to be collected; and
(4)最大限度减轻信息收集对答复者造成的负担,包括通过使用适当的自动化、电子、机械或其他技术收集手段或其他形式的信息技术,例如允许以电子方式提交答复。
(4) Minimize the burden of the collection of information on those who are to respond, including through the use of appropriate automated, electronic, mechanical, or other technological collection techniques or other forms of information technology, e.g., permitting electronic submission of responses.

第1.2条
§ 1.2
定义。
Definitions.
* * * * *
* * * * *
电子提交系指以USCIS为此目的所提供并批准的方式,以电子方式提交福利申请、佐证材料、文件、通知或通信,包括在线填写表格、通过网络门户、经由电子接口完成填写,或通过经批准的在线账户上传已填写完成表格的PDF文件。
E-filing means electronically filing or submitting a benefit request, supporting evidence, document, notice, or communication in a manner made available and approved by USCIS for that purpose, including by completing the form online, in a web portal, via an electronic interface, or by uploading a PDF of the completed form through an approved online account.

第103.2条
§ 103.2
福利申请的提交与裁决。
Submission and adjudication of benefit requests.
(a) * * *
(a) * * *
（一）准备和提交。（1）每份表格、申请福利或其他文件均须根据本节、本章其他适用规定以及国土安全部（DHS）为该申请指定的表格说明进行提交和签署。国土安全部可以规定申请福利的提交方式和格式，包括通过表格说明和相关材料，以落实和执行本节及其他适用法规所规定的要求。不符合适用法规、规章或表格说明的福利申请，可根据本节予以拒收或拒绝。如有任何不一致之处，以适用的法律和法规为准，国土安全部将以与这些法律和法规相一致的方式解释表格说明。
(1) Preparation and submission. (i) Every form, benefit request, or other document must be submitted and executed in accordance with this section, other applicable provisions of this chapter, and the form instructions designated by DHS for that request. DHS may prescribe the manner of submission and the format of benefit requests, including through form instructions and related materials, to implement and administer the requirements set forth in this section and other applicable regulations. A benefit request that does not comply with applicable statutes, regulations, or form instructions may be rejected or denied in accordance with this section. In the event of any inconsistency, the governing statute and regulations control, and DHS will construe form instructions in a manner consistent with those authorities.
（2）根据8 CFR 1.2的定义，向美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）提交的表格，除非根据本节第(a)(1)(iii)段予以豁免，否则必须以电子方式提交，适用情形如下：
(ii) A form filed with USCIS, as defined in 8 CFR 1.2, must be filed electronically, unless waived pursuant to paragraph (a)(1)(iii) of this section, where:
（A）该表格已可供电子提交超过180天；
(A) The form has been available for e-filing for more than 180 days;
（B）美国公民及移民服务局已自行决定强制要求以电子方式提交该表格（无论是一般情形还是特定情形）；以及
(B) USCIS has, in its discretion, mandated that the form be e-filed (either in general or in specified circumstances); and
（C）美国公民及移民服务局已在https://www.uscis.gov网站上提前60天发布通知，说明纸质表格将不再被接受、申请必须以电子方式提交的日期（无论是一般情形还是特定情形）。
(C) USCIS has published 60 days of advance notice on https://www.uscis.gov providing the date that the paper form will no longer be accepted and the request must be e-filed (either in general or in specified circumstances).
（3）美国公民及移民服务局可自行决定豁免强制电子提交的要求。申请福利者可通过提交《豁免电子提交要求申请书》来申请豁免强制电子提交的要求。
(iii) USCIS, in its discretion, may waive mandatory e-filing. Benefit requestors may request a waiver of mandatory e-filing by submitting a Request for Waiver of E-Filing Requirement.
（4）每份表格、福利申请或其他文件在提交时必须缴纳法规规定的费用。除本章另有规定外，费用必须在提出申请或提交申请时缴纳。
(iv) Each form, benefit request, or other document must be filed with the fee(s) required by regulation. Except as otherwise provided in this chapter, fees must be paid when the request is filed or submitted.
（5）申请费用一般不予退还，无论福利申请的结果如何，也无论裁决所需时间长短，是否退费由美国公民及移民服务局自行决定。
(v) Filing fees generally are non-refundable regardless of the outcome of the benefit request, or how much time the adjudication requires, and any decision to refund a fee is at the discretion of USCIS.

§ 106.2
§ 106.2
美国公民及移民服务局收费。
USCIS fees.

16. 历史上,“审查”(vetting)一词是指在作出资格决定之前,于福利申请裁决或处理期间所实施的背景审查程序。在持续审查制度下,政府持续监测在美外国人的风险指标,包括逾期滞留、犯罪行为、与特定团体的关联、未经授权的就业以及社交媒体活动。参见国土安全部,DHS/USCIS/PIA-076,《持续移民审查》(2019年2月14日),https://www.dhs.gov/​publication/​dhsuscispia-076-continuous-immigration-vetting。
16. Historically, vetting was the term associated with the background review process implemented during the adjudication or processing of a benefit request before an eligibility decision. Under continuous vetting, the Government continues to monitor aliens in the United States for risk indicators, including overstays, criminal conduct, ties to certain groups, unauthorized employment, and social media activity. See DHS, DHS/USCIS/PIA-076, Continuous Immigration Vetting (Feb. 14, 2019), https://www.dhs.gov/​publication/​dhsuscispia-076-continuous-immigration-vetting.

17. USCIS Lockbox staff is supported by a Case Resolution Unit staffed by USCIS employees in place to make decisions about the acceptability of a request where there is a question about whether to accept or reject a filing.

19. “在USCIS的用语中,‘拒收’(rejected)是指福利申请及费用因未能满足全部申报要求而被退回,未经全面审查,并可在正确完成后重新提交;而‘拒绝’(denied)则是指申请已经过完整裁决和审查,申请人被认定不符合所寻求福利的资格。”76 FR 53764, 53770(2011年8月29日)。另见USCIS《政策手册》第1卷《总体政策与程序》,B部分《福利申请的提交》,第6章《提交申请》,B节《受理处理》,https://www.uscis.gov/​policy-manual/​volume-1-part-b-chapter-6(截至2026年2月3日为现行版本)。
19. “In USCIS parlance, the term “rejected” means that the benefit request and fee payment are returned for failure to comply with all filing requirements without being fully considered, and can be re-filed when properly completed, while “denied” means that the request is fully adjudicated and considered, and the applicant is determined ineligible for the benefit sought.” 76 FR 53764, 53770 (Aug. 29, 2011). See also USCIS Policy Manual, Volume 1, General Policies and Procedures, Part B, Submission of Benefit Requests, Chapter 6, Submitting Requests, Section B, Intake Processing, https://www.uscis.gov/​policy-manual/​volume-1-part-b-chapter-6 (current as of Feb. 3, 2026).

20. USCIS的政策是,对于所有归化/入籍、永久或有条件居民身份、庇护或难民身份的福利申请,以及每份此类申请的附件和佐证文件,均保留原始来源移民记录,以备在诉讼中可能需要该原始来源记录作为证据之用。
20. USCIS policy is to maintain original source immigration records for all benefit requests for naturalization/citizenship, permanent or conditional resident status, asylum or refugee status, and each of these applications' supplements and supporting documents in the event the original source record may be needed as evidence during litigation.

48. H-1B非移民类别允许美国雇主临时雇用外国工人在美国从事专业职业的服务、与国防部合作研究与开发项目相关的特殊性质服务，或从事具有卓越才华或能力的时装模特工作。移民与国籍法第101(a)(15)(H)条，8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(15)(H)。
48. The H-1B nonimmigrant classification allows U.S. employers to temporarily employ foreign workers in the United States to perform services in a specialty occupation, services of an exceptional nature relating to a Department of Defense cooperative research and development project, or services as a fashion model of distinguished merit or ability. INA sec. 101(a)(15)(H), 8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(15)(H).
